## 任务：检查 4 个 stale RUNNING 实验 + 清理 registry

在 westd-17855 上检查这 4 个实验的进程是否存活，收集结果信息。

### 环境
- 服务器：root@westd-17855
- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/

### 检查列表

1. **interv_pred_14b_s42_fp32** — PID 191999
2. **interv_pred_a075_s42_fp32** — PID unknown
3. **alpha_sweep_a005_s42** — PID unknown
4. **alpha_sweep_a060_s42_fp32** — PID unknown

### 步骤

1. 检查 PID 是否存活：
```bash
ps -p 191999 -o pid,cmd,etime,stat 2>/dev/null || echo "PID 191999 DEAD"

# 搜索可能的进程
ps aux | grep -E "interv_pred_14b|interv_pred_a075|a005_s42|a060_s42_fp32" | grep -v grep
```

2. 对于已死的进程，检查是否有完整输出：
```bash
# interv_pred_14b_s42_fp32
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/predictive_te_14b_s42/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5
ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/predictive_te_14b_s42/analysis/ 2>/dev/null

# interv_pred_a075_s42_fp32
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/predictive_te_a075_s42_fp32/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5
# 也可能叫其他名字
ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/ | grep -i "pred.*a075\|interv.*a075"

# alpha_sweep_a005_s42
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/alpha_sweep_a005_s42/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5
ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/ | grep -i "a005"

# alpha_sweep_a060_s42_fp32
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/alpha_sweep_a060_s42_fp32/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5
ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/ | grep -i "a060_s42_fp32"
```

3. 对有完整输出的实验，检查 EXIT_CODE 或 all_metrics.json：
```bash
# 检查是否有最终的 all_metrics.json 或 gen_N 目录（N = num_gens-1）
for d in predictive_te_14b_s42 predictive_te_a075_s42_fp32; do
  echo "=== $d ==="
  ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/$d/gen_*/metrics/ 2>/dev/null | head -5
  cat /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/$d/EXIT_CODE 2>/dev/null || echo "no EXIT_CODE"
  ls /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/$d/all_metrics.json 2>/dev/null || echo "no all_metrics"
done
```

4. 用 read_registry 获取这 4 个实验的当前 registry 信息（hypothesis, metrics 等），然后对每个实验：
   - 进程已死 + 有完整输出 → update_registry status=DONE, conclusion=success（或 negative 根据结果）
   - 进程已死 + 无完整输出 → update_registry status=DONE, conclusion=failed
   - 进程仍存活 → 保持 RUNNING，汇报详情

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo).
